  <script>
function generateUUID() {
  var d = new Date().getTime();
  var uuid = 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(/[xy]/g, function(
    c
  ) {
    var r = ((d + Math.random() * 16) % 16) | 0;
    d = Math.floor(d / 16);
    return (c == 'x' ? r : (r & 0x7) | 0x8).toString(16);
  });
  return uuid;
}

var lzdDocCookies = {
  getItem: function(sKey) {
    return (
      decodeURIComponent(
        document.cookie.replace(
          new RegExp(
            '(?:(?:^|.*;)\\s*' +
              encodeURIComponent(sKey).replace(/[\-\.\+\*]/g, '\\$&') +
              '\\s*\\=\\s*([^;]*).*$)|^.*$'
          ),
          '$1'
        )
      ) || null
    );
  },
  setItem: function(sKey, sValue, vEnd, sPath, sDomain, bSecure) {
    if (!sKey || /^(?:expires|max\-age|path|domain|secure)$/i.test(sKey)) {
      return false;
    }
    var sExpires = '';
    var exdate = new Date();
    exdate.setDate(exdate.getDate() + vEnd);
    sExpires = ';expires=' + exdate.toGMTString();
    document.cookie =
      encodeURIComponent(sKey) +
      '=' +
      encodeURIComponent(sValue) +
      sExpires +
      (sDomain ? '; domain=' + sDomain : '') +
      (sPath ? '; path=' + sPath : '; path=/') +
      (bSecure ? '; secure' : '');
    return true;
  },
  hasItem: function(sKey) {
    if (!sKey) {
      return false;
    }
    return new RegExp(
      '(?:^|;\\s*)' +
        encodeURIComponent(sKey).replace(/[\-\.\+\*]/g, '\\$&') +
        '\\s*\\='
    ).test(document.cookie);
  },
  removeItem: function(sKey, sPath, sDomain) {
    if (!this.hasItem(sKey)) {
      return false;
    }
    document.cookie =
      encodeURIComponent(sKey) +
      '=; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT' +
      (sDomain ? '; domain=' + sDomain : '') +
      (sPath ? '; path=' + sPath : '; path=/');

    return true;
  }
};

var LZD_HOST_ARRAY = [
  '.lazada.co.id',
  '.lazada.com.my',
  '.lazada.com.ph',
  '.lazada.sg',
  '.lazada.co.th',
  '.lazada.vn',
  '.daraz.com.bd',
  '.daraz.lk',
  '.shop.com.mm',
  '.daraz.com.np',
  '.daraz.pk',
  '.lazada.test'
];
var currentDomain = '.lazada.sg';
var UUID = generateUUID();
var t_uid = lzdDocCookies.getItem('t_uid');
var anon_uid = lzdDocCookies.getItem('anon_uid');
for (var i = 0; i < LZD_HOST_ARRAY.length; i++) {
  if (window.location.host.indexOf(LZD_HOST_ARRAY[i]) > -1) {
    currentDomain = LZD_HOST_ARRAY[i];
  }
}

if (!lzdDocCookies.getItem('lzd_cid')) {
  lzdDocCookies.setItem('lzd_cid', UUID, 365, null, currentDomain);
}

if (!lzdDocCookies.getItem('t_uid')) {
  if (anon_uid) {
    lzdDocCookies.setItem('t_uid', anon_uid, 365, null, currentDomain);
  } else {
    lzdDocCookies.setItem('t_uid', UUID, 365, null, currentDomain);
  }
}

</script>

                                                                    <td width=" 105 ">
                                                                        <p style=" margin: 0 0 0px; ">
                                                                            <ol>
                                                                                <li>Do not register multiple accounts for the purposes of abusing any form of incentives or gaining undue advantage in any promotion, program or activity.</li><br>
                                                                                <li> Do not create accounts with fictitious, forged or compromised particulars (e.g. email address, contact number, delivery address, etc.). 
                                                                                    Do not use particulars that are owned by or pertain to other users or individuals.
                                                                                    </li><br>
                                                                                <li>Do not tamper any device software/ Daraz applications to install illicit software to clone Daraz applications or mask certain security features.</li>
                                                                            </ol>
                                                                        </p>

                                                                    <td width=" 105 ">
                                                                        <p style=" margin: 0 0 0px; ">
                                                                            <ol>
                                                                                <li>All agreements made in any of the official communication channels on Daraz, or any policies posted thereon, has to be honoured.</li><br>
                                                                                <li>
                                                                                    <p>Do not engage in any fraudulent activity. For example:</p>
                                                                                    <ul>
                                                                                        <li>Do not mislead or defraud Daraz and/or any other users of the Daraz Platform; and</li><br>
                                                                                        <li>Do not register accounts for the purpose of creating fake transactions.</li><br>
                                                                                    </ul>
                                                                                </li>
                                                                                <li>
                                                                                    <p>Keep all transactions within Daraz.</p>
                                                                                    <ul>
                                                                                        <li>All transactions originating from Daraz shall be completed within the Daraz Platform.</li><br>
                                                                                        <li>Do not engage in offline payments or transactions with other users of the Daraz Platform.</li><br>
                                                                                        <li>Do not lure other users to engage in offline transactions outside of the Daraz Platform. For your own safety and to protect against scams, do not accept requests to
                                                                                            engage in offline transactions outside of the Daraz Platform.</li><br>
                                                                                        <li>Do not redirect a sales transaction to other platforms and/or avenues.</li><br>
                                                                                        <li>All payments for Daraz transactions have to be completed within the Daraz Platform.</li><br>
                                                                                        <li>All exchange of messages and information should be done within the official communication channels available on the Daraz Platform.</li><br>
                                                                                    </ul>
                                                                                </li>
                                                                                <p>Daraz shall not be liable for any transaction made outside of the Daraz Platform.</p>
                                                                                <li>Do not provide a false or incomplete shipping address, or reject the acceptance of delivery. For example, if you see the product delivered is defective or not what you ordered
                                                                                    you should still accept the delivery and follow <a href="https://pages.daraz.pk/wow/i/pk/help/return-policy">
                                                        daraz return policy </a> </li>
                                                                            </ol>
                                                                        </p>
